Install Development Dependencies\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-2-install-development-dependencies\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2. Install Development Dependencies\" href=\"#2-install-development-dependencies\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Install basic Go dependencies\nmake deps\n\n# For full development (including linter)\nmake deps-dev\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Install basic Go dependencies\u003c/span\u003e\nmake deps\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e For full development (including linter)\u003c/span\u003e\nmake deps-dev\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRun \u003ccode\u003emake check-npm-1es\u003c/code\u003e to verify that every exact npm registry dependency in\nthe tracked lockfiles is available through the Microsoft 1ES public npm feed.\nThis downloads metadata and tarballs into a temporary cache, including dev,\ntransitive, and platform-specific optional dependencies, without installing\npackages, running lifecycle scripts, or changing npm configuration or lockfiles.\nRegistry entries without \u003ccode\u003eresolved\u003c/code\u003e are checked too; npm supports omitting that\nfield with \u003ca href=\"https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v11/using-npm/config#omit-lockfile-registry-resolved\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomit-lockfile-registry-resolved\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\nGit, local, and private-registry dependencies are reported separately and still\nrequire their own network access. The command fails if a registry package\ncannot be downloaded; caching an upstream version may require feed permissions.\nFailures report the unavailable pinned version and its lockfile locations;\nthe check does not search for replacements or suggest dependency upgrades.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3. \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw-actions\u003c/code\u003e, compile workflows against your fork or branch before the changes are released:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Compile against a fork with a specific branch or SHA\n./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \\\n  --actions-repo myorg/my-aw-actions \\\n  --action-tag my-feature-branch \\\n  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\n\n# Compile against the default repo pinned to a specific SHA\n./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \\\n  --action-tag abc123def456 \\\n  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Compile against a fork with a specific branch or SHA\u003c/span\u003e\n./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \\\n  --actions-repo myorg/my-aw-actions \\\n  --action-tag my-feature-branch \\\n  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Compile against the default repo pinned to a specific SHA\u003c/span\u003e\n./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \\\n  --action-tag abc123def456 \\\n  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFlags:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--action-mode action\u003c/code\u003e — Required when using \u003ccode\u003e--actions-repo\u003c/code\u003e. References actions as GitHub Actions from the external repository instead of inlining scripts locally.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--actions-repo \u0026lt;owner/repo\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e — Override the default \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw-actions\u003c/code\u003e repository (e.g., a personal fork).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--action-tag \u0026lt;tag-or-sha\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e — Pin action references to a specific tag, branch, or commit SHA.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhen to use\u003c/strong\u003e: Validating workflow compilation against a feature branch in \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw-actions\u003c/code\u003e before a release.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCompile against a specific gh-aw branch, tag, or SHA\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-compile-against-a-specific-gh-aw-branch-tag-or-sha\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Compile against a specific gh-aw branch, tag, or SHA\" href=\"#compile-against-a-specific-gh-aw-branch-tag-or-sha\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen developing changes to \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw\u003c/code\u003e itself, you can E2E-test the compiled workflows produced by an external repo against a specific revision of \u003ccode\u003egh-aw\u003c/code\u003e using the \u003ccode\u003e--gh-aw-ref\u003c/code\u003e convenience flag.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-alert markdown-alert-important\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cp class=\"markdown-alert-title\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-report mr-2\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M0 1.75C0 .784.784 0 1.75 0h12.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v9.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 13H8.06l-2.573 2.573A1.458 1.458 0 0 1 3 14.543V13H1.75A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 11.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v9.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h2a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.19l2.72-2.72a.749.749 0 0 1 .53-.22h6.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-9.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Zm7 2.25v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 9a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003eImportant\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003egh-aw\u003c/code\u003e binary you invoke should come from a \u003cstrong\u003eparallel checkout of \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw\u003c/code\u003e at the same ref\u003c/strong\u003e you pass to \u003ccode\u003e--gh-aw-ref\u003c/code\u003e. The flag only controls the refs emitted into the compiled \u003ccode\u003e.lock.yml\u003c/code\u003e files — it does \u003cem\u003enot\u003c/em\u003e fetch a different compiler. Compiling with one version of \u003ccode\u003egh-aw\u003c/code\u003e while emitting refs to a different version will silently mix incompatible compiler output and action scripts.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTypical end-to-end loop:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# 1. Check out github/gh-aw at the target ref in a sibling directory and build\ngit clone -b \u0026lt;REF\u0026gt; https://github.com/github/gh-aw.git ~/gh-aw \u0026amp;\u0026amp; make -C ~/gh-aw build\n\n# 2. From your downstream repo, compile workflows using that binary + ref\ncd ~/my-repo\n~/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref \u0026lt;REF\u0026gt; .github/workflows/*.md\n\n# 3. Commit the regenerated .lock.yml files and push so GitHub Actions runs them\ngit commit -am \u0026quot;Pin workflows to gh-aw@\u0026lt;REF\u0026gt; for latest fix\u0026quot;\ngit push\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 1. Check out github/gh-aw at the target ref in a sibling directory and build\u003c/span\u003e\ngit clone -b \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003eREF\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e https://github.com/github/gh-aw.git \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/gh-aw \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026amp;\u0026amp;\u003c/span\u003e make -C \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/gh-aw build\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 2. From your downstream repo, compile workflows using that binary + ref\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecd\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/my-repo\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003eREF\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e .github/workflows/\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e*\u003c/span\u003e.md\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 3. Commit the regenerated .lock.yml files and push so GitHub Actions runs them\u003c/span\u003e\ngit commit -am \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ePin workflows to gh-aw@\u0026lt;REF\u0026gt; for latest fix\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\ngit push\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eExamples of valid \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;REF\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e values:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Main branch\n~/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref main .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\n\n# Feature branch\n~/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref my-feature-branch .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\n\n# Specific commit SHA\n~/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref abc123def456 .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Main branch\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref main .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Feature branch\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref my-feature-branch .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Specific commit SHA\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref abc123def456 .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen a branch or tag name is supplied, the compiler resolves it to its commit SHA at compile time using the GitHub API, so the baked-in ref is immutable. Passing a full 40-character SHA skips the resolution call.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis emits action references of the form \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw/actions/setup@\u0026lt;SHA\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e in the compiled \u003ccode\u003e.lock.yml\u003c/code\u003e files. It is exactly equivalent to passing \u003ccode\u003e--action-mode release --action-tag \u0026lt;sha\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e and exists as a single, mnemonic flag for the \u003ccode\u003egh-aw\u003c/code\u003e-developer workflow.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhen to use\u003c/strong\u003e: Running a downstream workflow against an unreleased branch of \u003ccode\u003egh-aw\u003c/code\u003e to validate changes before merging. This flag is for developers of \u003ccode\u003egh-aw\u003c/code\u003e itself; end users should rely on released versions instead.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWatch and auto-compile workflows on changes\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca comparison\u003c/span\u003e\nmake bench-memory        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Memory profiling with pprof output\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhen to use\u003c/strong\u003e: Measuring compilation performance, detecting regressions, or optimizing.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInterpreting results\u003c/strong\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ens/op\u003c/strong\u003e: Nanoseconds per operation (lower is better)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eB/op\u003c/strong\u003e: Bytes allocated per operation (lower is better)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eallocs/op\u003c/strong\u003e: Number of allocations per operation (lower is better)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePerformance baselines\u003c/strong\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSimple workflows: \u0026lt;100ms compilation time\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eComplex workflows: \u0026lt;500ms compilation time\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMCP-heavy workflows: \u0026lt;1s compilation time\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eComparing benchmark results\u003c/strong\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell First time only - install dependencies\nmake deps deps-dev  # ~6-10min first time\n\n# 2. After making code changes - build\nmake build  # ~1.5s\n\n# 3. During development - fast feedback\nmake test-unit  # ~25s\n\n# 4. Before committing - comprehensive validation\nmake agent-finish  # ~10-15s\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 1. First time only - install dependencies\u003c/span\u003e\nmake deps deps-dev  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e ~6-10min first time\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 2. After making code changes - build\u003c/span\u003e\nmake build  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e ~1.5s\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 3. During development - fast feedback\u003c/span\u003e\nmake test-unit  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e ~25s\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 4. Before committing - comprehensive validation\u003c/span\u003e\nmake agent-finish  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e aria-label=\"Permalink: Schema changes not taking effect / stale schema errors\" href=\"#schema-changes-not-taking-effect--stale-schema-errors\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFiles under \u003ccode\u003epkg/parser/schemas/\u003c/code\u003e are embedded in the binary at compile time via\n\u003ccode\u003e//go:embed\u003c/code\u003e. Changing those JSON files without rebuilding means the running binary\nstill holds the old schema.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSolution\u003c/strong\u003e: Rebuild after any schema edit:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"make build\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003emake build\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRunning \u003ccode\u003ego test ./pkg/parser/...\u003c/code\u003e (or \u003ccode\u003emake test-unit\u003c/code\u003e) validates that every\nembedded schema is well-formed JSON via the \u003ccode\u003eTestEmbeddedSchemasAreValid\u003c/code\u003e test,\nbut it does \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e rebuild the \u003ccode\u003e./gh-aw\u003c/code\u003e CLI.  If you need the running CLI to\nreflect your schema edits, \u003ccode\u003emake build\u003c/code\u003e is still required.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003emake check-stale-schema-binary\u003c/code\u003e target (also run as part of \u003ccode\u003emake lint\u003c/code\u003e) uses\n\u003ccode\u003egit diff\u003c/code\u003e to detect schema-file changes and verifies the binary is up to date.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\"cannot find package\" \u003ccode\u003e.github/workflows/*.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMust have\u003c/strong\u003e an \u003ccode\u003eon:\u003c/code\u003e trigger that defines when they run\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCan be compiled directly with \u003ccode\u003e./gh-aw compile \u0026lt;workflow\u0026gt;.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExpected compilation rate\u003c/strong\u003e: 100% of standalone workflows should compile successfully\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eExample:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"---\non:\n  issues:\n    types: [opened]\nengine: copilot\n---\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e---\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eon\u003c/span\u003e:\n class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eengine\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ecopilot\u003c/span\u003e\n---\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" \u003ccode\u003e.github/workflows/shared/**/*.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDo NOT have\u003c/strong\u003e an \u003ccode\u003eon:\u003c/code\u003e trigger (they are partial configurations)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMeant to be imported using the \u003ccode\u003eimports:\u003c/code\u003e field in other workflows\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCannot be compiled directly\u003c/strong\u003e - this is expected behavior\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eExample shared workflow:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"---\nsafe-outputs:\n  app:\n    app-id: ${{ vars.APP_ID }}\n    private-key: ${{ secrets.APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}\n---\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e---\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003esafe-outputs\u003c/span\u003e:\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eapp\u003c/span\u003e:\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eapp-id\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e${{ vars.APP_ID }}\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eprivate-key\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e${{ secrets.APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}\u003c/span\u003e\n---\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo use a shared workflow:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"---\non:\n class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eimports\u003c/span\u003e:\n  - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e.github/workflows/shared/app-config.md\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eengine\u003c/span\u003e: workflows are reusable configuration fragments that:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProvide common configuration (e.g., safe-outputs, MCP server configs)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAre imported and merged into standalone workflows during compilation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDon't need triggers since they inherit context from the importing workflow\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you try to compile a shared workflow directly, you'll see a helpful error message explaining this pattern.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBuild Tools\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Download artifacts from a workflow run\ngh run download \u0026lt;run-id\u0026gt;\n\n# Extract slowest tests\ncat test-result-unit.json | jq -r 'select(.Action == \u0026quot;pass\u0026quot; and .Test != null) | \u0026quot;\\(.Elapsed)s \\(.Test)\u0026quot;' | sort -rn | head -20\n\n# Get package-level timing\ncat test-result-unit.json | jq -r 'select(.Action == \u0026quot;pass\u0026quot; and .Test == null) | \u0026quot;\\(.Elapsed)s \\(.Package)\u0026quot;' | sort -rn\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Download artifacts from a workflow run\u003c/span\u003e\ngh data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"package cli\n\nimport (\n    \u0026quot;fmt\u0026quot;\n    \u0026quot;os\u0026quot;\n    \n    \u0026quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/console\u0026quot;\n    \u0026quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/logger\u0026quot;\n    \u0026quot;github.com/spf13/cobra\u0026quot;\n)\n\n// Logger with namespace following cli:command_name convention\nvar commandLog = logger.New(\u0026quot;cli:command_name\u0026quot;)\n\n// NewCommandNameCommand creates the command\nfunc NewCommandNameCommand() *cobra.Command {\n    cmd := \u0026amp;cobra.Command{\n        Use:   \u0026quot;command-name \u0026lt;arg\u0026gt;\u0026quot;,\n        Short: \u0026quot;Brief description under 80 chars (no period)\u0026quot;,\n        Long: `Detailed description with context and examples.\n\nThis command:\n- Does something useful\n- Validates inputs\n- Provides helpful feedback\n\nExamples:\n  gh aw command-name arg              # Basic usage\n  gh aw command-name arg -v           # Verbose output\n  gh aw command-name arg --flag val   # With options`,\n        Args: cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n        RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {\n            // Parse flags\n            verbose, _ := cmd.Flags().GetBool(\u0026quot;verbose\u0026quot;)\n            flagValue, _ := cmd.Flags().GetString(\u0026quot;flag-name\u0026quot;)\n            \n            // Call main function\n            return RunCommandName(args[0], flagValue, verbose)\n        },\n    }\n    \n    // Add flags\n    cmd.Flags().StringP(\u0026quot;flag-name\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;f\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;default\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;Flag description\u0026quot;)\n    \n    return cmd\n}\n\n// RunCommandName executes the command logic (testable)\nfunc RunCommandName(arg string, flagValue string, verbose bool) error {\n    commandLog.Printf(\u0026quot;Starting: arg=%s, flag=%s\u0026quot;, arg, flagValue)\n    \n    // Validate inputs early\n    if arg == \u0026quot;\u0026quot; {\n        return fmt.Errorf(\u0026quot;argument cannot be empty\u0026quot;)\n    }\n    \n    // Execute logic\n    result, err := processCommand(arg, flagValue)\n    if err != nil {\n        commandLog.Printf(\u0026quot;Failed: %v\u0026quot;, err)\n        return fmt.Errorf(\u0026quot;failed to process: %w\u0026quot;, err)\n    }\n    \n    // Output results\n    fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatSuccessMessage(result))\n    \n    commandLog.Print(\u0026quot;Completed successfully\u0026quot;)\n    return nil\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"os\"\u003c/span\u003e\n    \n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/console\"\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/logger\"\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"github.com/spf13/cobra\"\u003c/span\u003e\n)\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// console.FormatInfoMessage(\u0026quot;Processing...\u0026quot;))\nfmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatWarningMessage(\u0026quot;Warning\u0026quot;))\nfmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatErrorMessage(err.Error()))\n\n// ❌ INCORRECT - Plain output, stdout\nfmt.Println(\u0026quot;Success\u0026quot;)\nfmt.Printf(\u0026quot;Status: %s\\n\u0026quot;, highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"func TestRunCommand(t *testing.T) {\n    tests := []struct {\n        name      string\n        input     string\n        expected  string\n        shouldErr bool\n    }{\n        {\n            name:      \u0026quot;valid input\u0026quot;,\n            input:     \u0026quot;test\u0026quot;,\n            expected:  \u0026quot;Success\u0026quot;,\n            shouldErr: false,\n        },\n        {\n            name:      \u0026quot;empty input\u0026quot;,\n            input:     \u0026quot;\u0026quot;,\n            shouldErr: true,\n        },\n        {\n            name:      \u0026quot;invalid format\u0026quot;,\n            input:     \u0026quot;invalid@\u0026quot;,\n            shouldErr: true,\n        },\n    }\n\n    for _, tt := range tests {\n        t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n            result, err := RunCommand(tt.input)\n            \n            if tt.shouldErr {\n                assert.Error(t, err)\n            } else {\n                assert.NoError(t, err)\n                assert.Equal(t, tt.expected, result)\n            }\n        })\n    }\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efunc\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eTestRunCommand\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003et\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e*\u003c/span\u003etesting.\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eT\u003c/span\u003e) {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etests\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e:=\u003c/span\u003e []\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003estruct\u003c/span\u003e {\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e      \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003estring\u003c/span\u003e\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003einput\u003c/span\u003e     \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003estring\u003c/span\u003e\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eexpected\u003c/span\u003e  \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003estring\u003c/span\u003e\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eshouldErr\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ebool\u003c/span\u003e\n    }{\n        {\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"test\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n            \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eexpected\u003c/span\u003e:  \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"Success\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e:      \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"empty input\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n            \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003einput\u003c/span\u003e:     \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n            \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eshouldErr\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etrue\u003c/span\u003e,\n        },\n        {\n class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etests\u003c/span\u003e {\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003et\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eRun\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ett\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eshouldErr\u003c/span\u003e {\n                \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eassert\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eError\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003et\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eerr\u003c/span\u003e)\n            } \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eexpected\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eresult\u003c/span\u003e)\n            }\n        })\n    }\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSpeed up linting by only checking changed files:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Lint changes since origin/main\nmake golint-incremental BASE_REF=origin/main\n\n# This is what CI uses on PRs - 50-75% faster!\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Lint changes since origin/main\u003c/span\u003e\nmake golint-incremental BASE_REF=origin/main\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e This is what CI uses on PRs - 50-75% faster!\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis runs the same incremental linting strategy as CI, checking only files changed since the base reference. It's particularly useful when working on pull requests where you want quick feedback on your changes without waiting for a full repository scan.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe incremental approach uses \u003ccode\u003egolangci-lint --new-from-rev\u003c/code\u003e to analyze only the files that differ from the specified base reference, providing significant performance improvements:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFull lint\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003emake lint\u003c/code\u003e): Scans entire repository\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIncremental lint\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003emake golint-incremental\u003c/code\u003e): Scans only changed files - typically 50-75% faster on PRs\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhen to use each approach:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse \u003ccode\u003emake golint-incremental BASE_REF=origin/main\u003c/code\u003e during development for fast feedback\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse \u003ccode\u003emake lint\u003c/code\u003e before final commits to ensure comprehensive coverage\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" file-specific exclusions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCWE mappings for compliance tracking\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDetailed rationale and mitigation strategies\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSuppression guidelines for \u003ccode\u003e#nosec\u003c/code\u003e annotations\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCompliance and audit trail Tips\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-development-tips\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Development Tips\" href=\"#development-tips\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse verbose testing\u003c/strong\u003e: \u003ccode\u003ego test -v\u003c/code\u003e for detailed output\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRun tests frequently\u003c/strong\u003e: Ensure changes don't break existing functionality\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCheck formatting\u003c/strong\u003e: Run \u003ccode\u003emake fmt\u003c/code\u003e before committing\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eValidate thoroughly\u003c/strong\u003e: Use \u003ccode\u003ego run test_validation.go\u003c/code\u003e before pull requests\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFollow PR lifecycle discipline\u003c/strong\u003e:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOpen PRs as \u003cstrong\u003edraft\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMove to \u003cstrong\u003eReady for review\u003c/strong\u003e and approve required CI workflows\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRun the \u003ccode\u003epr-finisher\u003c/code\u003e skill (automates final review/check/mergeability hardening) to get to green\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor features that deeply impact the engine, add the \u003ccode\u003esmoke\u003c/code\u003e label and approve workflows\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIf no smoke run is queued after setting \u003ccode\u003esmoke\u003c/code\u003e, or additional changes require another smoke run, toggle the \u003ccode\u003esmoke\u003c/code\u003e label (remove and re-add), then approve workflows again\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor changes to model routing (\u003ccode\u003eengine.model-routing\u003c/code\u003e), add the \u003ccode\u003esmoke-routing\u003c/code\u003e label and approve workflows. It runs nine routed smokes on the PR's code:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-copilot-routed\u003c/code\u003e: Copilot CLI with routing and no sub-agents. It also runs the default threat-detection engine on a pinned model, and a detection-job post-step checks that detection completed on that model without routing (\u003ccode\u003eD1\u003c/code\u003e–\u003ccode\u003eD3\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-claude-routed\u003c/code\u003e: Claude Code with routing on \u003ccode\u003emodel-provider: github\u003c/code\u003e; AWF selects \u003ccode\u003e/chat/completions\u003c/code\u003e and the main agent must use \u003ccode\u003e/v1/messages\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-pi-routed\u003c/code\u003e: pi with routing and two cross-family declared sub-agents (Claude and GPT)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-codex-routed\u003c/code\u003e: Codex with routing on \u003ccode\u003emodel-provider: github\u003c/code\u003e over \u003ccode\u003egpt-5.6-*\u003c/code\u003e candidates; the main agent must use \u003ccode\u003e/responses\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eH2\u003c/code\u003e fails unless the proxy-selected effort is \u003ccode\u003emax\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eH3\u003c/code\u003e fails unless every main-agent request the proxy recorded (\u003ccode\u003efirewall.model_routing\u003c/code\u003e \u003ccode\u003estage: request\u003c/code\u003e) has \u003ccode\u003erequestedEffort\u003c/code\u003e equal to the selected effort\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-copilot-sdk-routed\u003c/code\u003e: Copilot SDK with routing and a Claude sub-agent under a GPT main agent\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-copilot-routed-steering\u003c/code\u003e: Copilot CLI with routing on one model and a 7-credit \u003ccode\u003emax-ai-credits\u003c/code\u003e budget; the main agent must stop on the AWF AI-credit steering notice and finish under budget\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-copilot-routed-claude\u003c/code\u003e: Copilot CLI with routing over a Claude-only pool in \u003ccode\u003emode: auto\u003c/code\u003e; the main agent must use \u003ccode\u003e/chat/completions\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-pi-routed-claude\u003c/code\u003e: pi with routing over a Claude-only pool in \u003ccode\u003emode: robust\u003c/code\u003e; the main agent must use \u003ccode\u003e/v1/messages\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esmoke-copilot-sdk-routed-claude\u003c/code\u003e: Copilot SDK with routing over \u003ccode\u003eclaude-haiku-4.5\u003c/code\u003e (no reasoning effort) and a GPT sub-agent on \u003ccode\u003e/responses\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEach routed smoke's post-step (\u003ccode\u003eactions/setup/js/smoke_model_routing_assertions.cjs\u003c/code\u003e) checks runner- and proxy-written evidence, not the agent's reply. Every failed check logs its ID (\u003ccode\u003eR1\u003c/code\u003e–\u003ccode\u003eR4\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eS1\u003c/code\u003e–\u003ccode\u003eS4\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eM1\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eP1\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eE1\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eE2\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eU1\u003c/code\u003e–\u003ccode\u003eU3\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eH2\u003c/code\u003e for the Codex smoke, and \u003ccode\u003eT1\u003c/code\u003e–\u003ccode\u003eT4\u003c/code\u003e for the steering smoke) with the observed model, endpoint, status, event or steering notice, so the step log is enough to diagnose a failure\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eU1\u003c/code\u003e–\u003ccode\u003eU3\u003c/code\u003e check the unified session (\u003ccode\u003eusage/aw_session.jsonl\u003c/code\u003e) that audit, logs and footers read: \u003ccode\u003eU1\u003c/code\u003e no \u003ccode\u003esession.collection_warning\u003c/code\u003e; \u003ccode\u003eU2\u003c/code\u003e exactly one agent-phase \u003ccode\u003emodel.inventory\u003c/code\u003e (with \u003ccode\u003ecopilotInventory\u003c/code\u003e, a configured \u003ccode\u003ecopilot\u003c/code\u003e endpoint with models); \u003ccode\u003eU3\u003c/code\u003e one successful \u003ccode\u003emodel.resolution\u003c/code\u003e for each role in the smoke's explicit \u003ccode\u003eresolutionRoles\u003c/code\u003e. The unrouted engine smokes (\u003ccode\u003esmoke-copilot\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003esmoke-copilot-sdk\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003esmoke-claude\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003esmoke-claude-on-copilot\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003esmoke-codex\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003esmoke-pi\u003c/code\u003e) run \u003ccode\u003eE2\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eU1\u003c/code\u003e–\u003ccode\u003eU3\u003c/code\u003e alone through \u003ccode\u003esessionContractMain\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA routed smoke opts in to \u003ccode\u003eU1\u003c/code\u003e–\u003ccode\u003eU3\u003c/code\u003e by setting \u003ccode\u003eexpectModelInventory: true\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecopilotInventory: true\u003c/code\u003e or a \u003ccode\u003eresolutionRoles\u003c/code\u003e array (\u003ccode\u003e[]\u003c/code\u003e requires no roles); \u003ccode\u003efalse\u003c/code\u003e flags do not opt in. \u003ccode\u003eU2\u003c/code\u003e runs when \u003ccode\u003eexpectModelInventory\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003ecopilotInventory\u003c/code\u003e is true. If neither is true, the log explicitly reports \u003ccode\u003eU2 not evaluated\u003c/code\u003e. With \u003ccode\u003eexpectModelInventory: true\u003c/code\u003e, missing inventory evidence fails gracefully as \u003ccode\u003eFAIL U2 ... observed 0\u003c/code\u003e; it does not throw. A missing session instead fails \u003ccode\u003eE2\u003c/code\u003e and logs \u003ccode\u003eU1–U3 not evaluated\u003c/code\u003e. Unavailable evidence paths also fail \u003ccode\u003eE2\u003c/code\u003e. Repeated \u003ccode\u003eU1\u003c/code\u003e warnings are grouped by \u003ccode\u003e(code, path)\u003c/code\u003e with a count and an example line when available; every distinct pair remains visible\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003elabel_command\u003c/code\u003e removes the \u003ccode\u003esmoke-routing\u003c/code\u003e label when the runs start. To run them again, re-add the label, then approve workflows again\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe codebase uses a consistent pattern for GitHub API operations:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOne file per entity type\u003c/strong\u003e: \u003ccode\u003ecreate_issue.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecreate_pull_request.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecreate_discussion.go\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eConsistent structure\u003c/strong\u003e: Configuration parsing, validation, job generation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eParallel development\u003c/strong\u003e: Each creation type is independent\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExample Structure\u003c/strong\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"// In create_issue.go\ntype CreateIssuesConfig struct { ... }\nfunc (c *Compiler) parseCreateIssuesConfig(...) *CreateIssuesConfig\nfunc (c *Compiler) generateCreateIssuesJob(...) map[string]any\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// In create_issue.go\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003etype\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eCreateIssuesConfig\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003estruct\u003c/span\u003e { \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e...\u003c/span\u003e }\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efunc\u003c/span\u003e (\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ec\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e*\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eCompiler\u003c/span\u003e) \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eparseCreateIssuesConfig\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e...\u003c/span\u003e) \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e*\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eCreateIssuesConfig\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efunc\u003c/span\u003e (\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ec\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e*\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eCompiler\u003c/span\u003e) \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003egenerateCreateIssuesJob\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e...\u003c/span\u003e) \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003emap\u003c/span\u003e[\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003estring\u003c/span\u003e]\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eany\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2. Engine Architecture\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-2-engine-architecture\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2. Engine Architecture\" href=\"#2-engine-architecture\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEach AI engine follows a consistent pattern:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSeparate files\u003c/strong\u003e: \u003ccode\u003ecopilot_engine.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eclaude_engine.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecodex_engine.go\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eShared utilities\u003c/strong\u003e: \u003ccode\u003eengine_helpers.go\u003c/code\u003e contains common functionality\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClear interfaces\u003c/strong\u003e: All engines implement common methods\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKey Files\u003c/strong\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eagentic_engine.go\u003c/code\u003e - Base engine interface\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;engine\u0026gt;_engine.go\u003c/code\u003e - Engine-specific implementation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eengine_helpers.go\u003c/code\u003e - Shared helper functions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eengine_helpers_test.go\u003c/code\u003e - Common test utilities\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3. Compiler Architecture\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-3-compiler-architecture\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 3. Compiler Architecture\" href=\"#3-compiler-architecture\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe compiler is organized by responsibility:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecompiler.go\u003c/code\u003e - Main compilation orchestration\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecompiler_yaml.go\u003c/code\u003e - YAML generation logic\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecompiler_jobs.go\u003c/code\u003e - Job generation logic\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecompiler_test.go\u003c/code\u003e - Comprehensive test coverage\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis separation allows working on different aspects without conflicts.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e4. design:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAll expression-related logic in one file\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTree-based structure for complex conditions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eClean abstractions (ConditionNode interface)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eComprehensive tests in \u003ccode\u003eexpressions_test.go\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ \u003ccode\u003ecreate_issue_assignees_test.go\u003c/code\u003e - Clear purpose\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ \u003ccode\u003eengine_error_patterns_infinite_loop_test.go\u003c/code\u003e - Specific scenario\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e❌ \u003ccode\u003etest_utils.go\u003c/code\u003e - Too vague\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eContributing to dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFollow existing patterns\u003c/strong\u003e - Look for similar features first\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKeep files focused\u003c/strong\u003e - One responsibility per file\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse descriptive names\u003c/strong\u003e - Future you will thank present you\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWrite tests alongside\u003c/strong\u003e - Don't defer testing\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDocument patterns\u003c/strong\u003e - Update this guide when introducing new patterns\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor complete details, see \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/code-organization.md\"\u003eCode Organization Patterns\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 The process is:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e1. Prepare for Release\" href=\"#1-prepare-for-release\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Ensure you're on the main branch with latest changes\ngit checkout main\ngit pull origin main\n\n# Run all tests to ensure stability\nmake test\nmake lint\nmake fmt-check\n\n# Test build locally\nmake build-all\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Ensure you're on the main branch with latest changes\u003c/span\u003e\ngit checkout main\ngit pull origin main\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Run all tests to ensure stability\u003c/span\u003e\nmake \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etest\u003c/span\u003e\nmake lint\nmake fmt-check\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Test build locally\u003c/span\u003e\nmake build-all\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2. Create and Push Release Tag\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-2-create-and-push-release-tag\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2. Create and Push Release Tag\" href=\"#2-create-and-push-release-tag\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor patch releases (bug fixes), you can use the automated make target:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Automated patch release - finds current version and increments patch number\nmake patch-release\n\n# Automated patch release - finds current version and increments minor number\nmake minor-release\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Automated patch release - finds current version and increments patch number\u003c/span\u003e\nmake patch-release\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Automated patch release - finds current version and increments minor number\u003c/span\u003e\nmake minor-release\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOr create the tag manually:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Create a new tag following semantic versioning\n# Replace x.y.z with the actual version number\ngit tag -a v1.0.0 -m \u0026quot;Release v1.0.0\u0026quot;\n\n# Push the tag to trigger the release workflow\ngit push origin v1.0.0\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Create a new tag following semantic versioning\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Replace x.y.z with the actual version number\u003c/span\u003e\ngit tag -a v1.0.0 -m \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eRelease v1.0.0\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Push the tag to trigger the release workflow\u003c/span\u003e\ngit push origin v1.0.0\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3. Automated Release Process\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-3-automated-release-process\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 3. Automated Release Process\" href=\"#3-automated-release-process\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen you push a tag matching \u003ccode\u003ev*.*.*\u003c/code\u003e, GitHub Actions automatically:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRuns tests\u003c/strong\u003e to ensure code quality\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBuilds cross-platform binaries\u003c/strong\u003e using \u003ccode\u003egh-extension-precompile\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCreates GitHub release\u003c/strong\u003e with:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePre-compiled binaries for Linux (amd64, arm64)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePre-compiled binaries for macOS (amd64, arm64)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePre-compiled binaries for Windows (amd64)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAutomatic changelog generation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e4.
