<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Test my code changes quickly</h4><a id="user-content-test-my-code-changes-quickly" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Test my code changes quickly" href="#test-my-code-changes-quickly"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make test-unit  # Fast unit tests (~25s, recommended for development)"><pre>make test-unit  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Fast unit tests (~25s, recommended for development)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: During active development for rapid feedback on your changes.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run all tests before committing</h4><a id="user-content-run-all-tests-before-committing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run all tests before committing" href="#run-all-tests-before-committing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make test  # All tests including integration (~30s)"><pre>make <span class="pl-c1">test</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> All tests including integration (~30s)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Before creating a PR to ensure comprehensive validation.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Build the gh-aw binary</h4><a id="user-content-build-the-gh-aw-binary" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Build the gh-aw binary" href="#build-the-gh-aw-binary"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make build  # Includes sync-templates and sync-action-pins (~1.5s)"><pre>make build  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Includes sync-templates and sync-action-pins (~1.5s)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: After making code changes to compile the binary.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note</strong>: The build automatically syncs:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Templates from <code>.github/</code> to <code>pkg/cli/templates/</code></li>
<li>Action pins from <code>.github/aw/actions-lock.json</code> to <code>pkg/actionpins/data/action_pins.json</code> and <code>pkg/workflow/data/action_pins.json</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Validate everything before committing</h4><a id="user-content-validate-everything-before-committing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Validate everything before committing" href="#validate-everything-before-committing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make agent-finish  # Complete validation (~10-15s)"><pre>make agent-finish  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Complete validation (~10-15s)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>What it does</strong>: Runs deps-dev, fmt, lint, build, test-all, fix, recompile, dependabot, generate-schema-docs, generate-agent-factory, and security-scan.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Before committing to ensure everything passes CI checks.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Format my code</h4><a id="user-content-format-my-code" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Format my code" href="#format-my-code"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make fmt  # Format Go, JavaScript, and JSON files"><pre>make fmt  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Format Go, JavaScript, and JSON files</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Before committing or when linter reports formatting issues.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run the linter</h4><a id="user-content-run-the-linter" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run the linter" href="#run-the-linter"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make lint  # Full linting (includes format check) (~5.5s)"><pre>make lint  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Full linting (includes format check) (~5.5s)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: To catch code quality issues before committing.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run linter on only changed files (faster)</h4><a id="user-content-run-linter-on-only-changed-files-faster" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run linter on only changed files (faster)" href="#run-linter-on-only-changed-files-faster"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make golint-incremental BASE_REF=origin/main  # 50-75% faster on PRs"><pre>make golint-incremental BASE_REF=origin/main  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 50-75% faster on PRs</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: During development to get quick feedback on your changes.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Compile a specific workflow</h4><a id="user-content-compile-a-specific-workflow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Compile a specific workflow" href="#compile-a-specific-workflow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./gh-aw compile .github/workflows/my-workflow.md"><pre>./gh-aw compile .github/workflows/my-workflow.md</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Testing individual workflow compilation.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Compile against a different actions repository</h4><a id="user-content-compile-against-a-different-actions-repository" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Compile against a different actions repository" href="#compile-against-a-different-actions-repository"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When developing changes to <code>github/gh-aw-actions</code>, compile workflows against your fork or branch before the changes are released:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Compile against a fork with a specific branch or SHA
./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \
  --actions-repo myorg/my-aw-actions \
  --action-tag my-feature-branch \
  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md

# Compile against the default repo pinned to a specific SHA
./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \
  --action-tag abc123def456 \
  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Compile against a fork with a specific branch or SHA</span>
./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \
  --actions-repo myorg/my-aw-actions \
  --action-tag my-feature-branch \
  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Compile against the default repo pinned to a specific SHA</span>
./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \
  --action-tag abc123def456 \
  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Flags:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>--action-mode action</code> — Required when using <code>--actions-repo</code>. References actions as GitHub Actions from the external repository instead of inlining scripts locally.</li>
<li><code>--actions-repo &lt;owner/repo&gt;</code> — Override the default <code>github/gh-aw-actions</code> repository (e.g., a personal fork).</li>
<li><code>--action-tag &lt;tag-or-sha&gt;</code> — Pin action references to a specific tag, branch, or commit SHA.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Validating workflow compilation against a feature branch in <code>github/gh-aw-actions</code> before a release.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Compile against a specific gh-aw branch, tag, or SHA</h4><a id="user-content-compile-against-a-specific-gh-aw-branch-tag-or-sha" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Compile against a specific gh-aw branch, tag, or SHA" href="#compile-against-a-specific-gh-aw-branch-tag-or-sha"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When developing changes to <code>github/gh-aw</code> itself, you can E2E-test the compiled workflows produced by an external repo against a specific revision of <code>gh-aw</code> using the <code>--gh-aw-ref</code> convenience flag.</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-important" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-report mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 1.75C0 .784.784 0 1.75 0h12.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v9.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 13H8.06l-2.573 2.573A1.458 1.458 0 0 1 3 14.543V13H1.75A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 11.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v9.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h2a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.19l2.72-2.72a.749.749 0 0 1 .53-.22h6.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-9.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Zm7 2.25v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 9a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Important</p><p dir="auto">The <code>gh-aw</code> binary you invoke should come from a <strong>parallel checkout of <code>github/gh-aw</code> at the same ref</strong> you pass to <code>--gh-aw-ref</code>. The flag only controls the refs emitted into the compiled <code>.lock.yml</code> files — it does <em>not</em> fetch a different compiler. Compiling with one version of <code>gh-aw</code> while emitting refs to a different version will silently mix incompatible compiler output and action scripts.</p>
</div>
<p dir="auto">Typical end-to-end loop:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# 1. Check out github/gh-aw at the target ref in a sibling directory and build
git clone -b &lt;REF&gt; https://github.com/github/gh-aw.git ~/gh-aw &amp;&amp; make -C ~/gh-aw build

# 2. From your downstream repo, compile workflows using that binary + ref
cd ~/my-repo
~/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref &lt;REF&gt; .github/workflows/*.md

# 3. Commit the regenerated .lock.yml files and push so GitHub Actions runs them
git commit -am &quot;Pin workflows to gh-aw@&lt;REF&gt; for latest fix&quot;
git push"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 1. Check out github/gh-aw at the target ref in a sibling directory and build</span>
git clone -b <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>REF<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> https://github.com/github/gh-aw.git <span class="pl-k">~</span>/gh-aw <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> make -C <span class="pl-k">~</span>/gh-aw build

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 2. From your downstream repo, compile workflows using that binary + ref</span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> <span class="pl-k">~</span>/my-repo
<span class="pl-k">~</span>/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>REF<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> .github/workflows/<span class="pl-k">*</span>.md

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 3. Commit the regenerated .lock.yml files and push so GitHub Actions runs them</span>
git commit -am <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Pin workflows to gh-aw@&lt;REF&gt; for latest fix<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
git push</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Examples of valid <code>&lt;REF&gt;</code> values:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Main branch
~/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref main .github/workflows/my-workflow.md

# Feature branch
~/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref my-feature-branch .github/workflows/my-workflow.md

# Specific commit SHA
~/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref abc123def456 .github/workflows/my-workflow.md"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Main branch</span>
<span class="pl-k">~</span>/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref main .github/workflows/my-workflow.md

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Feature branch</span>
<span class="pl-k">~</span>/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref my-feature-branch .github/workflows/my-workflow.md

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Specific commit SHA</span>
<span class="pl-k">~</span>/gh-aw/gh-aw compile --gh-aw-ref abc123def456 .github/workflows/my-workflow.md</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">When a branch or tag name is supplied, the compiler resolves it to its commit SHA at compile time using the GitHub API, so the baked-in ref is immutable. Passing a full 40-character SHA skips the resolution call.</p>
<p dir="auto">This emits action references of the form <code>github/gh-aw/actions/setup@&lt;SHA&gt;</code> in the compiled <code>.lock.yml</code> files. It is exactly equivalent to passing <code>--action-mode release --action-tag &lt;sha&gt;</code> and exists as a single, mnemonic flag for the <code>gh-aw</code>-developer workflow.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Running a downstream workflow against an unreleased branch of <code>gh-aw</code> to validate changes before merging. This flag is for developers of <code>gh-aw</code> itself; end users should rely on released versions instead.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Watch and auto-compile workflows on changes</h4><a id="user-content-watch-and-auto-compile-workflows-on-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Watch and auto-compile workflows on changes" href="#watch-and-auto-compile-workflows-on-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make watch  # Or: ./gh-aw compile --watch"><pre>make watch  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or: ./gh-aw compile --watch</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Developing workflows with live reload.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Recompile all workflows after code changes</h4><a id="user-content-recompile-all-workflows-after-code-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Recompile all workflows after code changes" href="#recompile-all-workflows-after-code-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make recompile  # Recompile all .md workflows to .lock.yml"><pre>make recompile  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Recompile all .md workflows to .lock.yml</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: After modifying compiler code or workflow templates.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Critical</strong>: Always run this after changing workflow compilation logic.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install dependencies for the first time</h4><a id="user-content-install-dependencies-for-the-first-time" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install dependencies for the first time" href="#install-dependencies-for-the-first-time"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make deps      # Install Go and npm dependencies (~1.5min first run)
make deps-dev  # Add development tools like linter (~5-8min)"><pre>make deps      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Install Go and npm dependencies (~1.5min first run)</span>
make deps-dev  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Add development tools like linter (~5-8min)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Fresh clone setup or after dependency changes.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Clean build artifacts</h4><a id="user-content-clean-build-artifacts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Clean build artifacts" href="#clean-build-artifacts"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make clean  # Remove binaries, coverage files, security reports, etc."><pre>make clean  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Remove binaries, coverage files, security reports, etc.</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: To start fresh or troubleshoot build issues.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run security scans</h4><a id="user-content-run-security-scans" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run security scans" href="#run-security-scans"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make security-scan  # Run gosec and govulncheck"><pre>make security-scan  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run gosec and govulncheck</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Before releases or when checking for vulnerabilities.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run performance benchmarks</h4><a id="user-content-run-performance-benchmarks" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run performance benchmarks" href="#run-performance-benchmarks"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make bench               # Run all benchmarks (~30s)
make bench-compare       # Run with more iterations for benchstat comparison
make bench-memory        # Memory profiling with pprof output"><pre>make bench               <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run all benchmarks (~30s)</span>
make bench-compare       <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run with more iterations for benchstat comparison</span>
make bench-memory        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Memory profiling with pprof output</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Measuring compilation performance, detecting regressions, or optimizing.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Interpreting results</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>ns/op</strong>: Nanoseconds per operation (lower is better)</li>
<li><strong>B/op</strong>: Bytes allocated per operation (lower is better)</li>
<li><strong>allocs/op</strong>: Number of allocations per operation (lower is better)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Performance baselines</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Simple workflows: &lt;100ms compilation time</li>
<li>Complex workflows: &lt;500ms compilation time</li>
<li>MCP-heavy workflows: &lt;1s compilation time</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Comparing benchmark results</strong>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make bench                    # Baseline run, saves to bench_results.txt
# Make code changes
make bench-compare            # Comparison run, saves to bench_compare.txt
benchstat bench_results.txt bench_compare.txt  # Compare results"><pre>make bench                    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Baseline run, saves to bench_results.txt</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Make code changes</span>
make bench-compare            <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Comparison run, saves to bench_compare.txt</span>
benchstat bench_results.txt bench_compare.txt  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Compare results</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Memory profiling</strong>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make bench-memory                # Generate mem.prof and cpu.prof
go tool pprof -http=:8080 mem.prof  # Interactive memory analysis
go tool pprof -http=:8080 cpu.prof  # Interactive CPU analysis"><pre>make bench-memory                <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Generate mem.prof and cpu.prof</span>
go tool pprof -http=:8080 mem.prof  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Interactive memory analysis</span>
go tool pprof -http=:8080 cpu.prof  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Interactive CPU analysis</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Check for slow tests</h4><a id="user-content-check-for-slow-tests" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Check for slow tests" href="#check-for-slow-tests"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make test-perf  # Shows 10 slowest tests with timing"><pre>make test-perf  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Shows 10 slowest tests with timing</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Optimizing test suite performance.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Validate workflows with actionlint</h4><a id="user-content-validate-workflows-with-actionlint" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Validate workflows with actionlint" href="#validate-workflows-with-actionlint"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make actionlint  # Depends on build"><pre>make actionlint  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Depends on build</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Ensure compiled workflows are valid GitHub Actions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Update GitHub Actions to latest versions</h4><a id="user-content-update-github-actions-to-latest-versions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Update GitHub Actions to latest versions" href="#update-github-actions-to-latest-versions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make update  # Update actions, sync pins, rebuild"><pre>make update  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Update actions, sync pins, rebuild</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use</strong>: Updating action versions in workflows.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">The Golden Path</h3><a id="user-content-the-golden-path" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: The Golden Path" href="#the-golden-path"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For most development work, follow this sequence:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# 1. First time only - install dependencies
make deps deps-dev  # ~6-10min first time

# 2. After making code changes - build
make build  # ~1.5s

# 3. During development - fast feedback
make test-unit  # ~25s

# 4. Before committing - comprehensive validation
make agent-finish  # ~10-15s"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 1. First time only - install dependencies</span>
make deps deps-dev  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> ~6-10min first time</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 2. After making code changes - build</span>
make build  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> ~1.5s</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 3. During development - fast feedback</span>
make test-unit  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> ~25s</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 4. Before committing - comprehensive validation</span>
make agent-finish  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> ~10-15s</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">When to Use Each Test Target</h3><a id="user-content-when-to-use-each-test-target" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: When to Use Each Test Target" href="#when-to-use-each-test-target"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The project has several test targets optimized for different scenarios:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Target</th>
<th>Speed</th>
<th>What It Tests</th>
<th>Use When</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>test-unit</code></td>
<td>~25s</td>
<td>Unit tests only (excludes integration)</td>
<td><strong>Recommended</strong> for rapid iteration during development</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test</code></td>
<td>~30s</td>
<td>Unit + all integration tests</td>
<td>Before committing, comprehensive validation</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-integration-compile</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>Workflow compilation integration tests</td>
<td>Testing compiler changes specifically</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-integration-mcp-playwright</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>MCP Playwright integration</td>
<td>Testing Playwright MCP functionality</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-integration-mcp-other</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>Other MCP integration tests</td>
<td>Testing GitHub/Config MCP features</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-integration-logs</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>Log parsing and analysis</td>
<td>Testing log-related functionality</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-integration-workflow</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>Workflow package integration</td>
<td>Testing workflow compilation end-to-end</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-all</code></td>
<td>~30s</td>
<td>Go + JavaScript tests</td>
<td>Complete test coverage</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-js</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>JavaScript-only tests</td>
<td>Testing JS action code</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-security</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>Security regression tests</td>
<td>Validating security fixes</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-coverage</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>Tests with coverage report</td>
<td>Analyzing test coverage</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>test-perf</code></td>
<td>Varies</td>
<td>All tests + timing analysis</td>
<td>Finding slow tests</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Quick decision guide</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Developing a feature?</strong> → <code>make test-unit</code></li>
<li><strong>Ready to commit?</strong> → <code>make test</code> or <code>make agent-finish</code></li>
<li><strong>Changed compiler code?</strong> → <code>make test-integration-compile</code></li>
<li><strong>Working on JavaScript?</strong> → <code>make test-js</code></li>
<li><strong>Security-sensitive change?</strong> → <code>make test-security</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Expected Output and Timing</h3><a id="user-content-expected-output-and-timing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Expected Output and Timing" href="#expected-output-and-timing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Common Error Scenarios</h3><a id="user-content-common-error-scenarios" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Common Error Scenarios" href="#common-error-scenarios"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">"golangci-lint is not installed"</h4><a id="user-content-golangci-lint-is-not-installed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: &quot;golangci-lint is not installed&quot;" href="#golangci-lint-is-not-installed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Solution</strong>: Run <code>make deps-dev</code> to install development dependencies.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">"Node.js version X is not supported"</h4><a id="user-content-nodejs-version-x-is-not-supported" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: &quot;Node.js version X is not supported&quot;" href="#nodejs-version-x-is-not-supported"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Solution</strong>: Use the Dev Container or GitHub Codespace — generic dev environments are not supported (see <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md#prerequisites">CONTRIBUTING.md</a>).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Test failures after <code>git pull</code></h4><a id="user-content-test-failures-after-git-pull" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Test failures after git pull" href="#test-failures-after-git-pull"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Solution</strong>: Rebuild dependencies and binary:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make deps
make build
make test"><pre>make deps
make build
make <span class="pl-c1">test</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Workflows fail to compile</h4><a id="user-content-workflows-fail-to-compile" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Workflows fail to compile" href="#workflows-fail-to-compile"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Solution</strong>: Ensure you've built the latest binary and synced templates:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make build
make recompile"><pre>make build
make recompile</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Schema changes not taking effect / stale schema errors</h4><a id="user-content-schema-changes-not-taking-effect--stale-schema-errors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Schema changes not taking effect / stale schema errors" href="#schema-changes-not-taking-effect--stale-schema-errors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Files under <code>pkg/parser/schemas/</code> are embedded in the binary at compile time via
<code>//go:embed</code>. Changing those JSON files without rebuilding means the running binary
still holds the old schema.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Solution</strong>: Rebuild after any schema edit:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make build"><pre>make build</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Running <code>go test ./pkg/parser/...</code> (or <code>make test-unit</code>) validates that every
embedded schema is well-formed JSON via the <code>TestEmbeddedSchemasAreValid</code> test,
but it does <strong>not</strong> rebuild the <code>./gh-aw</code> CLI.  If you need the running CLI to
reflect your schema edits, <code>make build</code> is still required.</p>
<p dir="auto">The <code>make check-stale-schema-binary</code> target (also run as part of <code>make lint</code>) uses
<code>git diff</code> to detect schema-file changes and verifies the binary is up to date.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">"cannot find package" errors</h4><a id="user-content-cannot-find-package-errors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: &quot;cannot find package&quot; errors" href="#cannot-find-package-errors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Solution</strong>: Clean and reinstall dependencies:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make clean
make deps
make build"><pre>make clean
make deps
make build</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Workflow Compilation</h2><a id="user-content-workflow-compilation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Workflow Compilation" href="#workflow-compilation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Workflow Types and Compilation Expectations</h3><a id="user-content-workflow-types-and-compilation-expectations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Workflow Types and Compilation Expectations" href="#workflow-types-and-compilation-expectations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The repository contains two types of workflow files:</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Standalone Workflows (Main Workflows)</h4><a id="user-content-1-standalone-workflows-main-workflows" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Standalone Workflows (Main Workflows)" href="#1-standalone-workflows-main-workflows"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Located in <code>.github/workflows/*.md</code></li>
<li><strong>Must have</strong> an <code>on:</code> trigger that defines when they run</li>
<li>Can be compiled directly with <code>./gh-aw compile &lt;workflow&gt;.md</code></li>
<li><strong>Expected compilation rate</strong>: 100% of standalone workflows should compile successfully</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="---
on:
  issues:
    types: [opened]
engine: copilot
---"><pre>---
<span class="pl-ent">on</span>:
  <span class="pl-ent">issues</span>:
    <span class="pl-ent">types</span>: <span class="pl-s">[opened]</span>
<span class="pl-ent">engine</span>: <span class="pl-s">copilot</span>
---</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Shared Workflow Components</h4><a id="user-content-2-shared-workflow-components" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Shared Workflow Components" href="#2-shared-workflow-components"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Located in <code>.github/workflows/shared/**/*.md</code></li>
<li><strong>Do NOT have</strong> an <code>on:</code> trigger (they are partial configurations)</li>
<li>Meant to be imported using the <code>imports:</code> field in other workflows</li>
<li><strong>Cannot be compiled directly</strong> - this is expected behavior</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Example shared workflow:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="---
safe-outputs:
  app:
    app-id: ${{ vars.APP_ID }}
    private-key: ${{ secrets.APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}
---"><pre>---
<span class="pl-ent">safe-outputs</span>:
  <span class="pl-ent">app</span>:
    <span class="pl-ent">app-id</span>: <span class="pl-s">${{ vars.APP_ID }}</span>
    <span class="pl-ent">private-key</span>: <span class="pl-s">${{ secrets.APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}</span>
---</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">To use a shared workflow:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="---
on:
  issues:
    types: [opened]
imports:
  - .github/workflows/shared/app-config.md
engine: copilot
---"><pre>---
<span class="pl-ent">on</span>:
  <span class="pl-ent">issues</span>:
    <span class="pl-ent">types</span>: <span class="pl-s">[opened]</span>
<span class="pl-ent">imports</span>:
  - <span class="pl-s">.github/workflows/shared/app-config.md</span>
<span class="pl-ent">engine</span>: <span class="pl-s">copilot</span>
---</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Compilation Success Rate</h3><a id="user-content-compilation-success-rate" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Compilation Success Rate" href="#compilation-success-rate"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">As of the latest audit:</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why Shared Workflows Don't Compile</h3><a id="user-content-why-shared-workflows-dont-compile" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why Shared Workflows Don't Compile" href="#why-shared-workflows-dont-compile"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Shared workflows are reusable configuration fragments that:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Provide common configuration (e.g., safe-outputs, MCP server configs)</li>
<li>Are imported and merged into standalone workflows during compilation</li>
<li>Don't need triggers since they inherit context from the importing workflow</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">If you try to compile a shared workflow directly, you'll see a helpful error message explaining this pattern.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Build Tools</h2><a id="user-content-build-tools" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Build Tools" href="#build-tools"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project uses <code>tools.go</code> to track build-time tool dependencies. This ensures everyone uses the same tool versions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install Tools</h3><a id="user-content-install-tools" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install Tools" href="#install-tools"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make tools"><pre>make tools</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This installs all tools listed in <code>tools.go</code> at the versions specified in <code>go.mod</code>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>golangci-lint</strong>: Go linter with comprehensive checks</li>
<li><strong>actionlint</strong>: GitHub Actions workflow linter</li>
<li><strong>gosec</strong>: Go security linter</li>
<li><strong>gopls</strong>: Go language server for IDE support</li>
<li><strong>govulncheck</strong>: Go vulnerability scanner</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Adding a New Tool</h3><a id="user-content-adding-a-new-tool" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Adding a New Tool" href="#adding-a-new-tool"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Add blank import to <code>tools.go</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="_ &quot;github.com/example/tool/cmd/tool&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-s1">_</span> <span class="pl-s">"github.com/example/tool/cmd/tool"</span></pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Update dependencies:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="go get github.com/example/tool/cmd/tool@latest
go mod tidy"><pre>go get github.com/example/tool/cmd/tool@latest
go mod tidy</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Install: <code>make tools</code></p>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Tool Version Management</h3><a id="user-content-tool-version-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Tool Version Management" href="#tool-version-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Tool versions are locked in <code>go.mod</code> and <code>go.sum</code>, ensuring:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Consistency</strong>: Same tool versions in CI and local development</li>
<li><strong>Reproducibility</strong>: Tool versions are version-controlled</li>
<li><strong>Simplicity</strong>: Single command to install all tools</li>
<li><strong>Discoverability</strong>: <code>tools.go</code> shows all build tools at a glance</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Install the local version of gh-aw extension
make install

# Verify installation
gh aw --help"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Install the local version of gh-aw extension</span>
make install

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Verify installation</span>
gh aw --help</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Adding New Tests</h3><a id="user-content-adding-new-tests" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Adding New Tests" href="#adding-new-tests"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
