<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CI Test Artifacts</h3><a id="user-content-ci-test-artifacts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CI Test Artifacts" href="#ci-test-artifacts"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The CI workflow generates JSON test result artifacts with timing information that can be downloaded and analyzed:</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Available Artifacts</h4><a id="user-content-available-artifacts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Available Artifacts" href="#available-artifacts"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>test-result-unit.json</strong>: Unit test results with timing data</li>
<li><strong>test-result-integration-*.json</strong>: Integration test results for each test group</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">JSON Format</h4><a id="user-content-json-format" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: JSON Format" href="#json-format"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Each test result file contains newline-delimited JSON (ndjson) with test events:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{&quot;Time&quot;:&quot;2025-12-12T13:17:30Z&quot;,&quot;Action&quot;:&quot;pass&quot;,&quot;Package&quot;:&quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/logger&quot;,&quot;Elapsed&quot;:0.022}"><pre>{<span class="pl-ent">"Time"</span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>2025-12-12T13:17:30Z<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,<span class="pl-ent">"Action"</span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>pass<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,<span class="pl-ent">"Package"</span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/logger<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,<span class="pl-ent">"Elapsed"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">0.022</span>}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Key fields:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>Time</code>: ISO 8601 timestamp</li>
<li><code>Action</code>: Test event (<code>start</code>, <code>run</code>, <code>pass</code>, <code>fail</code>, <code>output</code>)</li>
<li><code>Package</code>: Go package being tested</li>
<li><code>Test</code>: Test name (if applicable)</li>
<li><code>Elapsed</code>: Test duration in seconds</li>
<li><code>Output</code>: Test output (for <code>output</code> actions)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Analyzing Test Timing</h4><a id="user-content-analyzing-test-timing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Analyzing Test Timing" href="#analyzing-test-timing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To extract timing information from artifacts:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Download artifacts from a workflow run
gh run download &lt;run-id&gt;

# Extract slowest tests
cat test-result-unit.json | jq -r 'select(.Action == &quot;pass&quot; and .Test != null) | &quot;\(.Elapsed)s \(.Test)&quot;' | sort -rn | head -20

# Get package-level timing
cat test-result-unit.json | jq -r 'select(.Action == &quot;pass&quot; and .Test == null) | &quot;\(.Elapsed)s \(.Package)&quot;' | sort -rn"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Download artifacts from a workflow run</span>
gh run download <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>run-id<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Extract slowest tests</span>
cat test-result-unit.json <span class="pl-k">|</span> jq -r <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>select(.Action == "pass" and .Test != null) | "\(.Elapsed)s \(.Test)"<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> sort -rn <span class="pl-k">|</span> head -20

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get package-level timing</span>
cat test-result-unit.json <span class="pl-k">|</span> jq -r <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>select(.Action == "pass" and .Test == null) | "\(.Elapsed)s \(.Package)"<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> sort -rn</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Mining Test Data</h4><a id="user-content-mining-test-data" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Mining Test Data" href="#mining-test-data"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The JSON format enables various analyses:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Identify slow tests across multiple runs</li>
<li>Track test performance trends over time</li>
<li>Detect flaky tests by comparing results</li>
<li>Generate test execution reports</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CLI Command Development</h2><a id="user-content-cli-command-development" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CLI Command Development" href="#cli-command-development"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When developing new CLI commands for <code>gh aw</code>, follow these established patterns and conventions to maintain consistency across the codebase.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start Guide</h3><a id="user-content-quick-start-guide" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start Guide" href="#quick-start-guide"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Create command file</strong>: <code>pkg/cli/command_name_command.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Create test file</strong>: <code>pkg/cli/command_name_command_test.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Follow the structure pattern</strong> (see below)</li>
<li><strong>Use standard flags</strong> from <code>flags.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Implement comprehensive tests</strong></li>
<li><strong>Run validation</strong>: <code>make agent-finish</code></li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Standard Command Structure</h3><a id="user-content-standard-command-structure" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Standard Command Structure" href="#standard-command-structure"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="package cli

import (
    &quot;fmt&quot;
    &quot;os&quot;
    
    &quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/console&quot;
    &quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/logger&quot;
    &quot;github.com/spf13/cobra&quot;
)

// Logger with namespace following cli:command_name convention
var commandLog = logger.New(&quot;cli:command_name&quot;)

// NewCommandNameCommand creates the command
func NewCommandNameCommand() *cobra.Command {
    cmd := &amp;cobra.Command{
        Use:   &quot;command-name &lt;arg&gt;&quot;,
        Short: &quot;Brief description under 80 chars (no period)&quot;,
        Long: `Detailed description with context and examples.

This command:
- Does something useful
- Validates inputs
- Provides helpful feedback

Examples:
  gh aw command-name arg              # Basic usage
  gh aw command-name arg -v           # Verbose output
  gh aw command-name arg --flag val   # With options`,
        Args: cobra.ExactArgs(1),
        RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
            // Parse flags
            verbose, _ := cmd.Flags().GetBool(&quot;verbose&quot;)
            flagValue, _ := cmd.Flags().GetString(&quot;flag-name&quot;)
            
            // Call main function
            return RunCommandName(args[0], flagValue, verbose)
        },
    }
    
    // Add flags
    cmd.Flags().StringP(&quot;flag-name&quot;, &quot;f&quot;, &quot;default&quot;, &quot;Flag description&quot;)
    
    return cmd
}

// RunCommandName executes the command logic (testable)
func RunCommandName(arg string, flagValue string, verbose bool) error {
    commandLog.Printf(&quot;Starting: arg=%s, flag=%s&quot;, arg, flagValue)
    
    // Validate inputs early
    if arg == &quot;&quot; {
        return fmt.Errorf(&quot;argument cannot be empty&quot;)
    }
    
    // Execute logic
    result, err := processCommand(arg, flagValue)
    if err != nil {
        commandLog.Printf(&quot;Failed: %v&quot;, err)
        return fmt.Errorf(&quot;failed to process: %w&quot;, err)
    }
    
    // Output results
    fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatSuccessMessage(result))
    
    commandLog.Print(&quot;Completed successfully&quot;)
    return nil
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">package</span> cli

<span class="pl-k">import</span> (
    <span class="pl-s">"fmt"</span>
    <span class="pl-s">"os"</span>
    
    <span class="pl-s">"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/console"</span>
    <span class="pl-s">"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/logger"</span>
    <span class="pl-s">"github.com/spf13/cobra"</span>
)

<span class="pl-c">// Logger with namespace following cli:command_name convention</span>
<span class="pl-k">var</span> <span class="pl-s1">commandLog</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">logger</span>.<span class="pl-c1">New</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cli:command_name"</span>)

<span class="pl-c">// NewCommandNameCommand creates the command</span>
<span class="pl-k">func</span> <span class="pl-s1">NewCommandNameCommand</span>() <span class="pl-c1">*</span>cobra.<span class="pl-smi">Command</span> {
    <span class="pl-s1">cmd</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> <span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span>cobra.<span class="pl-smi">Command</span>{
        <span class="pl-s1">Use</span>:   <span class="pl-s">"command-name &lt;arg&gt;"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">Short</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Brief description under 80 chars (no period)"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">Long</span>: <span class="pl-s">`Detailed description with context and examples.</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s">This command:</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Does something useful</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Validates inputs</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Provides helpful feedback</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s">Examples:</span>
<span class="pl-s">  gh aw command-name arg              # Basic usage</span>
<span class="pl-s">  gh aw command-name arg -v           # Verbose output</span>
<span class="pl-s">  gh aw command-name arg --flag val   # With options`</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">Args</span>: <span class="pl-s1">cobra</span>.<span class="pl-c1">ExactArgs</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>),
        <span class="pl-s1">RunE</span>: <span class="pl-k">func</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cmd</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span>cobra.<span class="pl-smi">Command</span>, <span class="pl-s1">args</span> []<span class="pl-smi">string</span>) <span class="pl-smi">error</span> {
            <span class="pl-c">// Parse flags</span>
            <span class="pl-s1">verbose</span>, <span class="pl-s1">_</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> <span class="pl-s1">cmd</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Flags</span>().<span class="pl-c1">GetBool</span>(<span class="pl-s">"verbose"</span>)
            <span class="pl-s1">flagValue</span>, <span class="pl-s1">_</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> <span class="pl-s1">cmd</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Flags</span>().<span class="pl-c1">GetString</span>(<span class="pl-s">"flag-name"</span>)
            
            <span class="pl-c">// Call main function</span>
            <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">RunCommandName</span>(<span class="pl-s1">args</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>], <span class="pl-s1">flagValue</span>, <span class="pl-s1">verbose</span>)
        },
    }
    
    <span class="pl-c">// Add flags</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">cmd</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Flags</span>().<span class="pl-c1">StringP</span>(<span class="pl-s">"flag-name"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"f"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"default"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Flag description"</span>)
    
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">cmd</span>
}

<span class="pl-c">// RunCommandName executes the command logic (testable)</span>
<span class="pl-k">func</span> <span class="pl-s1">RunCommandName</span>(<span class="pl-s1">arg</span> <span class="pl-smi">string</span>, <span class="pl-s1">flagValue</span> <span class="pl-smi">string</span>, <span class="pl-s1">verbose</span> <span class="pl-smi">bool</span>) <span class="pl-smi">error</span> {
    <span class="pl-s1">commandLog</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Printf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Starting: arg=%s, flag=%s"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">arg</span>, <span class="pl-s1">flagValue</span>)
    
    <span class="pl-c">// Validate inputs early</span>
    <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">arg</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">""</span> {
        <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Errorf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"argument cannot be empty"</span>)
    }
    
    <span class="pl-c">// Execute logic</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">result</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> <span class="pl-s1">processCommand</span>(<span class="pl-s1">arg</span>, <span class="pl-s1">flagValue</span>)
    <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">err</span> <span class="pl-c1">!=</span> <span class="pl-c1">nil</span> {
        <span class="pl-s1">commandLog</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Printf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Failed: %v"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span>)
        <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Errorf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"failed to process: %w"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span>)
    }
    
    <span class="pl-c">// Output results</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Fprintln</span>(<span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Stderr</span>, <span class="pl-s1">console</span>.<span class="pl-c1">FormatSuccessMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s1">result</span>))
    
    <span class="pl-s1">commandLog</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Completed successfully"</span>)
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-c1">nil</span>
}</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Naming Conventions</h3><a id="user-content-naming-conventions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Naming Conventions" href="#naming-conventions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Element</th>
<th>Pattern</th>
<th>Example</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Command file</strong></td>
<td><code>*_command.go</code></td>
<td><code>audit_command.go</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Test file</strong></td>
<td><code>*_command_test.go</code></td>
<td><code>audit_command_test.go</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Logger</strong></td>
<td><code>cli:command_name</code></td>
<td><code>logger.New("cli:audit")</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Constructor</strong></td>
<td><code>NewXCommand()</code></td>
<td><code>NewAuditCommand()</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Runner</strong></td>
<td><code>RunX(...)</code></td>
<td><code>RunAuditWorkflowRun(...)</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Config</strong></td>
<td><code>XConfig</code></td>
<td><code>AuditConfig</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Standard Flags</h3><a id="user-content-standard-flags" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Standard Flags" href="#standard-flags"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use helper functions from <code>flags.go</code> for consistency:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import &quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/cli&quot;

// Add common flags
addEngineFlag(cmd)          // --engine/-e (Override AI engine)
addRepoFlag(cmd)            // --repo/-r (Target repository)
addOutputFlag(cmd, dir)     // --output/-o (Output directory)
addJSONFlag(cmd)            // --json/-j (JSON output)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s">"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/cli"</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Add common flags</span>
<span class="pl-s1">addEngineFlag</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cmd</span>)          <span class="pl-c">// --engine/-e (Override AI engine)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">addRepoFlag</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cmd</span>)            <span class="pl-c">// --repo/-r (Target repository)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">addOutputFlag</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cmd</span>, <span class="pl-s1">dir</span>)     <span class="pl-c">// --output/-o (Output directory)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">addJSONFlag</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cmd</span>)            <span class="pl-c">// --json/-j (JSON output)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Reserved short flags</strong>: <code>-v</code> (verbose), <code>-e</code> (engine), <code>-r</code> (repo), <code>-o</code> (output), <code>-j</code> (json), <code>-f</code> (force/file), <code>-w</code> (watch)</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Output and Error Handling</h3><a id="user-content-output-and-error-handling" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Output and Error Handling" href="#output-and-error-handling"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>All output must go to stderr</strong> (except JSON):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// ✅ CORRECT - Console formatted, stderr
fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatSuccessMessage(&quot;Success&quot;))
fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatInfoMessage(&quot;Processing...&quot;))
fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatWarningMessage(&quot;Warning&quot;))
fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatErrorMessage(err.Error()))

// ❌ INCORRECT - Plain output, stdout
fmt.Println(&quot;Success&quot;)
fmt.Printf(&quot;Status: %s\n&quot;, status)"><pre><span class="pl-c">// ✅ CORRECT - Console formatted, stderr</span>
<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Fprintln</span>(<span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Stderr</span>, <span class="pl-s1">console</span>.<span class="pl-c1">FormatSuccessMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Success"</span>))
<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Fprintln</span>(<span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Stderr</span>, <span class="pl-s1">console</span>.<span class="pl-c1">FormatInfoMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Processing..."</span>))
<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Fprintln</span>(<span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Stderr</span>, <span class="pl-s1">console</span>.<span class="pl-c1">FormatWarningMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Warning"</span>))
<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Fprintln</span>(<span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Stderr</span>, <span class="pl-s1">console</span>.<span class="pl-c1">FormatErrorMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s1">err</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Error</span>()))

<span class="pl-c">// ❌ INCORRECT - Plain output, stdout</span>
<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Println</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Success"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Printf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Status: %s<span class="pl-cce">\n</span>"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">status</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Error wrapping with context</strong>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// ✅ CORRECT - Context + wrapping
if err != nil {
    return fmt.Errorf(&quot;failed to process workflow: %w&quot;, err)
}

// ❌ INCORRECT - No context
if err != nil {
    return err
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// ✅ CORRECT - Context + wrapping</span>
<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">err</span> <span class="pl-c1">!=</span> <span class="pl-c1">nil</span> {
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Errorf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"failed to process workflow: %w"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span>)
}

<span class="pl-c">// ❌ INCORRECT - No context</span>
<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">err</span> <span class="pl-c1">!=</span> <span class="pl-c1">nil</span> {
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">err</span>
}</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Testing Requirements</h3><a id="user-content-testing-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Testing Requirements" href="#testing-requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Every command needs comprehensive table-driven tests:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="func TestRunCommand(t *testing.T) {
    tests := []struct {
        name      string
        input     string
        expected  string
        shouldErr bool
    }{
        {
            name:      &quot;valid input&quot;,
            input:     &quot;test&quot;,
            expected:  &quot;Success&quot;,
            shouldErr: false,
        },
        {
            name:      &quot;empty input&quot;,
            input:     &quot;&quot;,
            shouldErr: true,
        },
        {
            name:      &quot;invalid format&quot;,
            input:     &quot;invalid@&quot;,
            shouldErr: true,
        },
    }

    for _, tt := range tests {
        t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
            result, err := RunCommand(tt.input)
            
            if tt.shouldErr {
                assert.Error(t, err)
            } else {
                assert.NoError(t, err)
                assert.Equal(t, tt.expected, result)
            }
        })
    }
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">func</span> <span class="pl-s1">TestRunCommand</span>(<span class="pl-s1">t</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span>testing.<span class="pl-smi">T</span>) {
    <span class="pl-s1">tests</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> []<span class="pl-k">struct</span> {
        <span class="pl-c1">name</span>      <span class="pl-smi">string</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">input</span>     <span class="pl-smi">string</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">expected</span>  <span class="pl-smi">string</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">shouldErr</span> <span class="pl-smi">bool</span>
    }{
        {
            <span class="pl-s1">name</span>:      <span class="pl-s">"valid input"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s1">input</span>:     <span class="pl-s">"test"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s1">expected</span>:  <span class="pl-s">"Success"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s1">shouldErr</span>: <span class="pl-c1">false</span>,
        },
        {
            <span class="pl-s1">name</span>:      <span class="pl-s">"empty input"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s1">input</span>:     <span class="pl-s">""</span>,
            <span class="pl-s1">shouldErr</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span>,
        },
        {
            <span class="pl-s1">name</span>:      <span class="pl-s">"invalid format"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s1">input</span>:     <span class="pl-s">"invalid@"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s1">shouldErr</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span>,
        },
    }

    <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">_</span>, <span class="pl-s1">tt</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> <span class="pl-k">range</span> <span class="pl-s1">tests</span> {
        <span class="pl-s1">t</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Run</span>(<span class="pl-s1">tt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">name</span>, <span class="pl-k">func</span>(<span class="pl-s1">t</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span>testing.<span class="pl-smi">T</span>) {
            <span class="pl-s1">result</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> <span class="pl-s1">RunCommand</span>(<span class="pl-s1">tt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">input</span>)
            
            <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">tt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">shouldErr</span> {
                <span class="pl-s1">assert</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Error</span>(<span class="pl-s1">t</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span>)
            } <span class="pl-k">else</span> {
                <span class="pl-s1">assert</span>.<span class="pl-c1">NoError</span>(<span class="pl-s1">t</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span>)
                <span class="pl-s1">assert</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Equal</span>(<span class="pl-s1">t</span>, <span class="pl-s1">tt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">expected</span>, <span class="pl-s1">result</span>)
            }
        })
    }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Test coverage requirements</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Valid inputs</li>
<li>Invalid inputs (empty, malformed, out-of-range)</li>
<li>Edge cases (nil, empty arrays, boundary values)</li>
<li>Flag combinations</li>
<li>Error conditions</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Help Text Guidelines</h3><a id="user-content-help-text-guidelines" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Help Text Guidelines" href="#help-text-guidelines"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Short description</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Under 80 characters</li>
<li>Action-oriented (starts with verb)</li>
<li>No period at the end</li>
<li>Clear and concise</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Long description</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Overview (what it does)</li>
<li>Context (when to use)</li>
<li>Details (behavior, options)</li>
<li>Minimum 3 practical examples</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Include WorkflowIDExplanation</strong> for workflow commands:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import &quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/cli&quot;

Long: `Description...

` + cli.WorkflowIDExplanation + `

Examples:
  ...`,"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s">"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/cli"</span>

<span class="pl-s1">Long</span>: <span class="pl-s">`Description...</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s">`</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-s1">cli</span>.<span class="pl-c1">WorkflowIDExplanation</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-s">`</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s">Examples:</span>
<span class="pl-s">  ...`</span>,</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Command Development Checklist</h3><a id="user-content-command-development-checklist" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Command Development Checklist" href="#command-development-checklist"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When creating a new command, verify:</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> File named <code>*_command.go</code> in <code>pkg/cli/</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Logger: <code>logger.New("cli:command_name")</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> <code>NewXCommand()</code> and <code>RunX()</code> functions defined</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Short description &lt; 80 chars, no period</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Long description with 3+ examples</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Standard flags used where applicable</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Input validation implemented early</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Console formatting for all output</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> All output to stderr (except JSON)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Errors wrapped with context</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Test file <code>*_command_test.go</code> created</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Table-driven tests with multiple scenarios</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Valid, invalid, and edge case tests</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> <code>make test-unit</code> passes</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> <code>make agent-finish</code> passes</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">File Organization Patterns</h3><a id="user-content-file-organization-patterns" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: File Organization Patterns" href="#file-organization-patterns"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Simple commands</strong> (&lt; 500 lines): Single file</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="command_name_command.go
command_name_command_test.go"><pre class="notranslate"><code>command_name_command.go
command_name_command_test.go
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Complex commands</strong> (&gt; 500 lines): Split into focused files</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="command_name_command.go       # Command definition
command_name_config.go        # Configuration types
command_name_helpers.go       # Utility functions
command_name_validation.go    # Validation logic
command_name_orchestrator.go  # Main orchestration"><pre class="notranslate"><code>command_name_command.go       # Command definition
command_name_config.go        # Configuration types
command_name_helpers.go       # Utility functions
command_name_validation.go    # Validation logic
command_name_orchestrator.go  # Main orchestration
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Comprehensive Documentation</h3><a id="user-content-comprehensive-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Comprehensive Documentation" href="#comprehensive-documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For complete command development patterns including:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Anti-patterns to avoid</li>
<li>Complete examples with tests</li>
<li>Error handling best practices</li>
<li>Advanced patterns for complex commands</li>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Fix formatting issues

# Address linter warnings

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Local Incremental Linting</h3><a id="user-content-local-incremental-linting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Local Incremental Linting" href="#local-incremental-linting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Speed up linting by only checking changed files:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Lint changes since origin/main
make golint-incremental BASE_REF=origin/main

# This is what CI uses on PRs - 50-75% faster!"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Lint changes since origin/main</span>
make golint-incremental BASE_REF=origin/main

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> This is what CI uses on PRs - 50-75% faster!</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This runs the same incremental linting strategy as CI, checking only files changed since the base reference. It's particularly useful when working on pull requests where you want quick feedback on your changes without waiting for a full repository scan.</p>
<p dir="auto">The incremental approach uses <code>golangci-lint --new-from-rev</code> to analyze only the files that differ from the specified base reference, providing significant performance improvements:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Full lint</strong> (<code>make lint</code>): Scans entire repository</li>
<li><strong>Incremental lint</strong> (<code>make golint-incremental</code>): Scans only changed files - typically 50-75% faster on PRs</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use each approach:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Use <code>make golint-incremental BASE_REF=origin/main</code> during development for fast feedback</li>
<li>Use <code>make lint</code> before final commits to ensure comprehensive coverage</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security Scanning</h2><a id="user-content-security-scanning" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security Scanning" href="#security-scanning"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The project includes automated security scanning to detect vulnerabilities, code smells, and dependency issues.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Running Security Scans Locally</h3><a id="user-content-running-security-scans-locally" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Running Security Scans Locally" href="#running-security-scans-locally"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Run all security scans (gosec, govulncheck)
make security-scan

# Run individual scans
make security-gosec      # Go security linter
make security-govulncheck # Go vulnerability database check"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run all security scans (gosec, govulncheck)</span>
make security-scan

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run individual scans</span>
make security-gosec      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Go security linter</span>
make security-govulncheck <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Go vulnerability database check</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security Scan Tools</h3><a id="user-content-security-scan-tools" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security Scan Tools" href="#security-scan-tools"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>gosec</strong>: Static analysis tool for Go that detects security issues in source code</li>
<li><strong>govulncheck</strong>: Official Go tool that checks for known vulnerabilities in dependencies</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Interpreting Results</h3><a id="user-content-interpreting-results" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Interpreting Results" href="#interpreting-results"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Gosec Results</h4><a id="user-content-gosec-results" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Gosec Results" href="#gosec-results"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Results are saved to <code>gosec-report.json</code></li>
<li>Review findings by severity (HIGH, MEDIUM, LOW)</li>
<li>False positives can be suppressed with <code>// #nosec G&lt;rule-id&gt;</code> comments</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Govulncheck Results</h4><a id="user-content-govulncheck-results" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Govulncheck Results" href="#govulncheck-results"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Shows vulnerabilities in direct and indirect dependencies</li>
<li>Indicates if vulnerable code paths are actually called</li>
<li>Update affected dependencies to resolve issues</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Suppressing False Positives</h3><a id="user-content-suppressing-false-positives" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Suppressing False Positives" href="#suppressing-false-positives"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CI/CD Integration</h3><a id="user-content-cicd-integration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CI/CD Integration" href="#cicd-integration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Security scans run automatically on:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Daily scheduled scan (6:00 AM UTC)</li>
<li>Manual workflow dispatch</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Results are uploaded to the GitHub Security tab in SARIF format.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security Scanning Exclusions</h3><a id="user-content-security-scanning-exclusions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security Scanning Exclusions" href="#security-scanning-exclusions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">This documentation provides:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Complete list of global and file-specific exclusions</li>
<li>CWE mappings for compliance tracking</li>
<li>Detailed rationale and mitigation strategies</li>
