</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Development Tips</h3><a id="user-content-development-tips" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Development Tips" href="#development-tips"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Use verbose testing</strong>: <code>go test -v</code> for detailed output</li>
<li><strong>Run tests frequently</strong>: Ensure changes don't break existing functionality</li>
<li><strong>Check formatting</strong>: Run <code>make fmt</code> before committing</li>
<li><strong>Validate thoroughly</strong>: Use <code>go run test_validation.go</code> before pull requests</li>
<li><strong>Follow PR lifecycle discipline</strong>:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Open PRs as <strong>draft</strong></li>
<li>Move to <strong>Ready for review</strong> and approve required CI workflows</li>
<li>Run the <code>pr-finisher</code> skill (automates final review/check/mergeability hardening) to get to green</li>
<li>For features that deeply impact the engine, add the <code>smoke</code> label and approve workflows</li>
<li>If no smoke run is queued after setting <code>smoke</code>, or additional changes require another smoke run, toggle the <code>smoke</code> label (remove and re-add), then approve workflows again</li>
<li>For changes to model routing (<code>engine.model-routing</code>), add the <code>smoke-routing</code> label and approve workflows. It runs nine routed smokes on the PR's code:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>smoke-copilot-routed</code>: Copilot CLI with routing and no sub-agents. It also runs the default threat-detection engine on a pinned model, and a detection-job post-step checks that detection completed on that model without routing (<code>D1</code>–<code>D3</code>)</li>
<li><code>smoke-claude-routed</code>: Claude Code with routing on <code>model-provider: github</code>; AWF selects <code>/chat/completions</code> and the main agent must use <code>/v1/messages</code></li>
<li><code>smoke-pi-routed</code>: pi with routing and two cross-family declared sub-agents (Claude and GPT)</li>
<li><code>smoke-codex-routed</code>: Codex with routing on <code>model-provider: github</code> over <code>gpt-5.6-*</code> candidates; the main agent must use <code>/responses</code>, <code>H2</code> fails unless the proxy-selected effort is <code>max</code>, and <code>H3</code> fails unless every main-agent request the proxy recorded (<code>firewall.model_routing</code> <code>stage: request</code>) has <code>requestedEffort</code> equal to the selected effort</li>
<li><code>smoke-copilot-sdk-routed</code>: Copilot SDK with routing and a Claude sub-agent under a GPT main agent</li>
<li><code>smoke-copilot-routed-steering</code>: Copilot CLI with routing on one model and a 7-credit <code>max-ai-credits</code> budget; the main agent must stop on the AWF AI-credit steering notice and finish under budget</li>
<li><code>smoke-copilot-routed-claude</code>: Copilot CLI with routing over a Claude-only pool in <code>mode: auto</code>; the main agent must use <code>/chat/completions</code></li>
<li><code>smoke-pi-routed-claude</code>: pi with routing over a Claude-only pool in <code>mode: robust</code>; the main agent must use <code>/v1/messages</code></li>
<li><code>smoke-copilot-sdk-routed-claude</code>: Copilot SDK with routing over <code>claude-haiku-4.5</code> (no reasoning effort) and a GPT sub-agent on <code>/responses</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Each routed smoke's post-step (<code>actions/setup/js/smoke_model_routing_assertions.cjs</code>) checks runner- and proxy-written evidence, not the agent's reply. Every failed check logs its ID (<code>R1</code>–<code>R4</code>, <code>S1</code>–<code>S4</code>, <code>M1</code>, <code>P1</code>, <code>E1</code>, <code>E2</code>, <code>U1</code>–<code>U3</code>, <code>H2</code> for the Codex smoke, and <code>T1</code>–<code>T4</code> for the steering smoke) with the observed model, endpoint, status, event or steering notice, so the step log is enough to diagnose a failure</li>
<li><code>U1</code>–<code>U3</code> check the unified session (<code>usage/aw_session.jsonl</code>) that audit, logs and footers read: <code>U1</code> no <code>session.collection_warning</code>; <code>U2</code> exactly one agent-phase <code>model.inventory</code> (with <code>copilotInventory</code>, a configured <code>copilot</code> endpoint with models); <code>U3</code> one successful <code>model.resolution</code> for each role in the smoke's explicit <code>resolutionRoles</code>. The unrouted engine smokes (<code>smoke-copilot</code>, <code>smoke-copilot-sdk</code>, <code>smoke-claude</code>, <code>smoke-claude-on-copilot</code>, <code>smoke-codex</code>, <code>smoke-pi</code>) run <code>E2</code> and <code>U1</code>–<code>U3</code> alone through <code>sessionContractMain</code></li>
<li>A routed smoke opts in to <code>U1</code>–<code>U3</code> by setting <code>expectModelInventory: true</code>, <code>copilotInventory: true</code> or a <code>resolutionRoles</code> array (<code>[]</code> requires no roles); <code>false</code> flags do not opt in. <code>U2</code> runs when <code>expectModelInventory</code> or <code>copilotInventory</code> is true. If neither is true, the log explicitly reports <code>U2 not evaluated</code>. With <code>expectModelInventory: true</code>, missing inventory evidence fails gracefully as <code>FAIL U2 ... observed 0</code>; it does not throw. A missing session instead fails <code>E2</code> and logs <code>U1–U3 not evaluated</code>. Unavailable evidence paths also fail <code>E2</code>. Repeated <code>U1</code> warnings are grouped by <code>(code, path)</code> with a count and an example line when available; every distinct pair remains visible</li>
<li><code>label_command</code> removes the <code>smoke-routing</code> label when the runs start. To run them again, re-add the label, then approve workflows again</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Release Process</h2><a id="user-content-release-process" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Release Process" href="#release-process"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Architectural Patterns</h2><a id="user-content-architectural-patterns" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Architectural Patterns" href="#architectural-patterns"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Understanding the architectural patterns used in this codebase will help you make consistent contributions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Core Design Patterns</h3><a id="user-content-core-design-patterns" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Core Design Patterns" href="#core-design-patterns"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Create Functions Pattern</h4><a id="user-content-1-create-functions-pattern" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Create Functions Pattern" href="#1-create-functions-pattern"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The codebase uses a consistent pattern for GitHub API operations:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>One file per entity type</strong>: <code>create_issue.go</code>, <code>create_pull_request.go</code>, <code>create_discussion.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Consistent structure</strong>: Configuration parsing, validation, job generation</li>
<li><strong>Parallel development</strong>: Each creation type is independent</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Example Structure</strong>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// In create_issue.go
type CreateIssuesConfig struct { ... }
func (c *Compiler) parseCreateIssuesConfig(...) *CreateIssuesConfig
func (c *Compiler) generateCreateIssuesJob(...) map[string]any"><pre><span class="pl-c">// In create_issue.go</span>
<span class="pl-k">type</span> <span class="pl-smi">CreateIssuesConfig</span> <span class="pl-k">struct</span> { <span class="pl-c1">...</span> }
<span class="pl-k">func</span> (<span class="pl-s1">c</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-smi">Compiler</span>) <span class="pl-c1">parseCreateIssuesConfig</span>(<span class="pl-c1">...</span>) <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-smi">CreateIssuesConfig</span>
<span class="pl-k">func</span> (<span class="pl-s1">c</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-smi">Compiler</span>) <span class="pl-c1">generateCreateIssuesJob</span>(<span class="pl-c1">...</span>) <span class="pl-k">map</span>[<span class="pl-smi">string</span>]<span class="pl-smi">any</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Engine Architecture</h4><a id="user-content-2-engine-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Engine Architecture" href="#2-engine-architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Each AI engine follows a consistent pattern:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Separate files</strong>: <code>copilot_engine.go</code>, <code>claude_engine.go</code>, <code>codex_engine.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Shared utilities</strong>: <code>engine_helpers.go</code> contains common functionality</li>
<li><strong>Clear interfaces</strong>: All engines implement common methods</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Key Files</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>agentic_engine.go</code> - Base engine interface</li>
<li><code>&lt;engine&gt;_engine.go</code> - Engine-specific implementation</li>
<li><code>engine_helpers.go</code> - Shared helper functions</li>
<li><code>engine_helpers_test.go</code> - Common test utilities</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Compiler Architecture</h4><a id="user-content-3-compiler-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Compiler Architecture" href="#3-compiler-architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The compiler is organized by responsibility:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>compiler.go</code> - Main compilation orchestration</li>
<li><code>compiler_yaml.go</code> - YAML generation logic</li>
<li><code>compiler_jobs.go</code> - Job generation logic</li>
<li><code>compiler_test.go</code> - Comprehensive test coverage</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">This separation allows working on different aspects without conflicts.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Expression Building</h4><a id="user-content-4-expression-building" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Expression Building" href="#4-expression-building"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The expression system (<code>expressions.go</code>) demonstrates cohesive design:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>All expression-related logic in one file</li>
<li>Tree-based structure for complex conditions</li>
<li>Clean abstractions (ConditionNode interface)</li>
<li>Comprehensive tests in <code>expressions_test.go</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">File Organization Best Practices</h3><a id="user-content-file-organization-best-practices" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: File Organization Best Practices" href="#file-organization-best-practices"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">✅ Good Patterns</h4><a id="user-content--good-patterns" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ✅ Good Patterns" href="#-good-patterns"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Focused files</strong>: Each file has a clear, single responsibility</li>
<li><strong>Descriptive names</strong>: File names clearly indicate their purpose</li>
<li><strong>Collocated tests</strong>: Tests live next to implementation</li>
<li><strong>Reasonable size</strong>: Most files under 500 lines</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">❌ Anti-Patterns to Avoid</h4><a id="user-content--anti-patterns-to-avoid" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ❌ Anti-Patterns to Avoid" href="#-anti-patterns-to-avoid"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>God files</strong>: Single file doing too many things</li>
<li><strong>Vague naming</strong>: <code>utils.go</code>, <code>helpers.go</code> without context</li>
<li><strong>Mixed concerns</strong>: Unrelated functionality in one file</li>
<li><strong>Massive tests</strong>: All tests in one huge file</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">When to Create New Files</h3><a id="user-content-when-to-create-new-files" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: When to Create New Files" href="#when-to-create-new-files"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use this decision tree:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>New safe output type?</strong> → <code>create_&lt;entity&gt;.go</code></li>
<li><strong>New AI engine?</strong> → <code>&lt;engine&gt;_engine.go</code></li>
<li><strong>New domain feature?</strong> → <code>&lt;feature&gt;.go</code></li>
<li><strong>File over 800 lines?</strong> → Consider splitting</li>
<li><strong>Independent functionality?</strong> → Create new file</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Code Organization Guidelines</h3><a id="user-content-code-organization-guidelines" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Code Organization Guidelines" href="#code-organization-guidelines"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">File Size Targets</h4><a id="user-content-file-size-targets" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: File Size Targets" href="#file-size-targets"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Small</strong> (50-200 lines): Simple utilities, helpers</li>
<li><strong>Medium</strong> (200-500 lines): Feature implementations</li>
<li><strong>Large</strong> (500-800 lines): Complex features</li>
<li><strong>Very Large</strong> (800+ lines): Core infrastructure only</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Naming Conventions</h4><a id="user-content-naming-conventions-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Naming Conventions" href="#naming-conventions-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Create operations</strong>: <code>create_&lt;entity&gt;.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Engines</strong>: <code>&lt;engine&gt;_engine.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Features</strong>: <code>&lt;feature&gt;.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Helpers</strong>: <code>&lt;subsystem&gt;_helpers.go</code></li>
<li><strong>Tests</strong>: <code>&lt;feature&gt;_test.go</code>, <code>&lt;feature&gt;_integration_test.go</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Package Structure</h3><a id="user-content-package-structure" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Package Structure" href="#package-structure"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Testing Architecture</h3><a id="user-content-testing-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Testing Architecture" href="#testing-architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Test Organization</h4><a id="user-content-test-organization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Test Organization" href="#test-organization"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Unit tests</strong>: <code>feature_test.go</code> - Fast, focused tests</li>
<li><strong>Integration tests</strong>: <code>feature_integration_test.go</code> - Cross-component tests</li>
<li><strong>Scenario tests</strong>: <code>feature_scenario_test.go</code> - Specific use cases</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Test Naming</h4><a id="user-content-test-naming" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Test Naming" href="#test-naming"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use descriptive names that explain what's being tested:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>✅ <code>create_issue_assignees_test.go</code> - Clear purpose</li>
<li>✅ <code>engine_error_patterns_infinite_loop_test.go</code> - Specific scenario</li>
<li>❌ <code>test_utils.go</code> - Too vague</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing to Architecture</h3><a id="user-content-contributing-to-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing to Architecture" href="#contributing-to-architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When adding new features:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Follow existing patterns</strong> - Look for similar features first</li>
<li><strong>Keep files focused</strong> - One responsibility per file</li>
<li><strong>Use descriptive names</strong> - Future you will thank present you</li>
<li><strong>Write tests alongside</strong> - Don't defer testing</li>
<li><strong>Document patterns</strong> - Update this guide when introducing new patterns</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">For complete details, see <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/code-organization.md">Code Organization Patterns</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Prerequisites for Releases</h3><a id="user-content-prerequisites-for-releases" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Prerequisites for Releases" href="#prerequisites-for-releases"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Before creating a release, ensure you have:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Maintainer access</strong> to the GitHub repository</li>
<li><strong>Push permissions</strong> to create tags</li>
<li><strong>Write access</strong> to GitHub releases</li>
<li><strong>All tests passing</strong> on the main branch</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Release Types</h3><a id="user-content-release-types" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Release Types" href="#release-types"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The project uses semantic versioning (semver):</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Major</strong> (v2.0.0): Breaking API changes, incompatible updates</li>
<li><strong>Minor</strong> (v1.1.0): New features, backward compatible</li>
<li><strong>Patch</strong> (v1.0.1): Bug fixes, backward compatible</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Official Release Process</h3><a id="user-content-official-release-process" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Official Release Process" href="#official-release-process"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Releases are <strong>automatically handled by GitHub Actions</strong> when you create a git tag. The process is:</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Prepare for Release</h4><a id="user-content-1-prepare-for-release" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Prepare for Release" href="#1-prepare-for-release"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Ensure you're on the main branch with latest changes
git checkout main
git pull origin main

# Run all tests to ensure stability
make test
make lint
make fmt-check

# Test build locally
make build-all"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Ensure you're on the main branch with latest changes</span>
git checkout main
git pull origin main

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run all tests to ensure stability</span>
make <span class="pl-c1">test</span>
make lint
make fmt-check

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Test build locally</span>
make build-all</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Create and Push Release Tag</h4><a id="user-content-2-create-and-push-release-tag" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Create and Push Release Tag" href="#2-create-and-push-release-tag"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For patch releases (bug fixes), you can use the automated make target:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Automated patch release - finds current version and increments patch number
make patch-release

# Automated patch release - finds current version and increments minor number
make minor-release"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Automated patch release - finds current version and increments patch number</span>
make patch-release

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Automated patch release - finds current version and increments minor number</span>
make minor-release</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or create the tag manually:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Create a new tag following semantic versioning
# Replace x.y.z with the actual version number
git tag -a v1.0.0 -m &quot;Release v1.0.0&quot;

# Push the tag to trigger the release workflow
git push origin v1.0.0"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create a new tag following semantic versioning</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Replace x.y.z with the actual version number</span>
git tag -a v1.0.0 -m <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Release v1.0.0<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Push the tag to trigger the release workflow</span>
git push origin v1.0.0</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Automated Release Process</h4><a id="user-content-3-automated-release-process" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Automated Release Process" href="#3-automated-release-process"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When you push a tag matching <code>v*.*.*</code>, GitHub Actions automatically:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Runs tests</strong> to ensure code quality</li>
<li><strong>Builds cross-platform binaries</strong> using <code>gh-extension-precompile</code></li>
<li><strong>Creates GitHub release</strong> with:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Pre-compiled binaries for Linux (amd64, arm64)</li>
<li>Pre-compiled binaries for macOS (amd64, arm64)</li>
<li>Pre-compiled binaries for Windows (amd64)</li>
<li>Automatic changelog generation</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Verify Release</h4><a id="user-content-4-verify-release" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Verify Release" href="#4-verify-release"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">After the GitHub Actions workflow completes:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Check the release was created successfully
gh release list

# Remove any existing extension
gh extension remove gh-aw || true

# Test installation as a GitHub CLI extension
gh extension install github/gh-aw@v1.0.0
gh aw --help"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Check the release was created successfully</span>
gh release list

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Remove any existing extension</span>
gh extension remove gh-aw <span class="pl-k">||</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Test installation as a GitHub CLI extension</span>
gh extension install github/gh-aw@v1.0.0
gh aw --help</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Release Workflow Details</h3><a id="user-content-release-workflow-details" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Release Workflow Details" href="#release-workflow-details"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The release is orchestrated by <code>.github/workflows/release.yml</code> which:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Triggers on</strong>: Git tags matching <code>v*.*.*</code> pattern or manual workflow dispatch</li>
<li><strong>Runs on</strong>: Ubuntu latest with Go version from <code>go.mod</code></li>
<li><strong>Permissions</strong>: Contents (write), packages (write), ID token (write)</li>
<li><strong>Artifacts</strong>: Cross-platform binaries, Docker images, checksums</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Rollback Process</h3><a id="user-content-rollback-process" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Rollback Process" href="#rollback-process"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If a release has critical issues:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Immediate</strong>: Delete the problematic release from GitHub</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="gh release delete v1.0.0 --yes
git tag -d v1.0.0
git push origin :refs/tags/v1.0.0"><pre>gh release delete v1.0.0 --yes
git tag -d v1.0.0
git push origin :refs/tags/v1.0.0</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Long-term</strong>: Create a new release with fixes</p>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Current Release Infrastructure Status</h3><a id="user-content-current-release-infrastructure-status" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Current Release Infrastructure Status" href="#current-release-infrastructure-status"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The project has a complete automated release system in place:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>✅ <strong>GitHub Actions workflow</strong> (<code>.github/workflows/release.yml</code>)</li>
<li>✅ <strong>Cross-platform binary builds</strong> via <code>gh-extension-precompile</code></li>
<li>✅ <strong>Semantic versioning</strong> with git tags</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The release system is <strong>production-ready</strong> and uses GitHub's official <code>gh-extension-precompile</code> action, which is the recommended approach for GitHub CLI extensions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Release Notes and Changelog</h3><a id="user-content-release-notes-and-changelog" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Release Notes and Changelog" href="#release-notes-and-changelog"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Release notes are automatically generated from:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Commit messages</strong> between releases</li>
<li><strong>Pull request titles</strong> and descriptions</li>
<li><strong>Conventional commit format</strong> is recommended for better changelog generation</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">To improve changelog quality, use conventional commit messages:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git commit -m &quot;feat: add new workflow command&quot;
git commit -m &quot;fix: resolve path handling on Windows&quot;
git commit -m &quot;docs: update installation instructions&quot;"><pre>git commit -m <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>feat: add new workflow command<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
git commit -m <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>fix: resolve path handling on Windows<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
git commit -m <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>docs: update installation instructions<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Version Management</h3><a id="user-content-version-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Version Management" href="#version-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Version information</strong> is automatically injected at build time</li>
<li><strong>Current version</strong> comes from git tags (<code>git describe --tags</code>)</li>
<li><strong>No manual version files</strong> need to be updated</li>
<li><strong>Build metadata</strong> includes commit hash and build date</li>
</ul>

</react-app>




  </div>

</turbo-frame>

    </main>
  </div>
